			}
			event_target:dtp_log2_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log3_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_3 value = dtp_logcode_2 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_3 value = dtp_logres_2 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log2_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log3_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log1_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_2 value = dtp_logcode_1 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_2 value = dtp_logres_1 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log1_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_tgt
			}
			from = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_1 value = dtp_rc }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_1 value = dtp_rr }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_last_code value = dtp_rc }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_last_ok value = dtp_rr }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_t
				}
				event_target:dtp_t = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			else = {
				from = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			log = "DTP | code [From.dtp_rc] | result [From.dtp_rr]"
		}
	}

					exists = event_target:dtp_t
				}
				event_target:dtp_t = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			else = {
				from = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			log = "DTP | code [From.dtp_rc] | result [From.dtp_rr]"
		}
	}

					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_4 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_4 value = 0 }
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log5_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log5_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_5 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_5 value = 0 }
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_6 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_6 value = 0 }
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log7_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log7_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_7 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_7 value = 0 }
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log8_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log8_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_8 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_8 value = 0 }
				}
			}
			event_target:dtp_log7_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log8_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_8 value = dtp_logcode_7 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_8 value = dtp_logres_7 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log7_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log8_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log6_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log7_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_7 value = dtp_logcode_6 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_7 value = dtp_logres_6 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log6_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log7_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log5_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_6 value = dtp_logcode_5 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_6 value = dtp_logres_5 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log5_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log4_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log5_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_5 value = dtp_logcode_4 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_5 value = dtp_logres_4 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log4_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log5_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log3_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log4_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_4 value = dtp_logcode_3 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_4 value = dtp_logres_3 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log3_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log4_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log2_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log3_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_3 value = dtp_logcode_2 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_3 value = dtp_logres_2 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log2_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log3_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log1_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_2 value = dtp_logcode_1 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_2 value = dtp_logres_1 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log1_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_tgt
			}
			from = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_1 value = dtp_rc }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_1 value = dtp_rr }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_last_code value = dtp_rc }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_last_ok value = dtp_rr }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_t
				}
				event_target:dtp_t = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			else = {
				from = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			log = "DTP | code [From.dtp_rc] | result [From.dtp_rr]"
		}
	}
}
